  the ordinary trait bound rustc 1.98 elaborates it to. This repairs core's
  `Option::as_deref` (`option.rs:1386`). The only shared change is
  `Environment::assumes_trait` (`types/parameters/environment.rs`).
- After a crate-wide declaration pass fails, an analysis-only census still runs
  every later pass and records each one's first error as a `lookahead` record
  that follows the terminal record. These records change no count and may
  derive from the failure.
- A declaration whose last field is or ends in a trait object has no static
  layout to demand. This repairs core's `Request` (`error.rs:588`), which the
  lookahead found.
- 22 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle, and
  all agree. Their three prefix amendments are recorded.
- Every PA1 to PA24 suite passes, with PA20 at 1451/1451.
- What remains:
  - `ptr/mod.rs:2592`: coherence cannot rule out the builtin-only `FnPtr`
    for a generic non-fundamental self type. A reduction with a one-element
    tuple `PartialEq` impl gives the same message, and rustc 1.98.0 accepts
    it. The record does not name core's competing impl. Lane A owns it, as
    the census frontier.
  - `<H::Inner as Deref>::Target` inside a nominal argument still fails with
    "invalid canonical trait selection": the unscoped builtins proof cannot
    restore the caller's lifetimes after normalization. rustc 1.98.0 accepts
    it; census 10 has no record of it. It joins Lane B's queue after the
    nightly repair.

Lane C [raw-pointer and slice type rows](parallel/raw-pointer-slice-rows.md) is
joined on main. Version 13 carries `*const T`, `*mut T` and
`[T]` in the integrator's allocated type recipe tags: 12 is `[u8 12][u8
mutable][id pointee]` and 13 is `[u8 13][id element]`. Tags 14 and 15 stay
reserved. A raw pointer has no region and admits an unsized pointee. No
sizedness travels: validation rejects a `str` or slice element by name, and
every consumer proves a slice element sized from its own facts. A forged
slice of a `?Sized` parameter passes validation and is rejected by the fresh
consumer. Older profiles keep main's rejection by name and their exact bytes.
Seven clients run natively and exit 42. This is the first prerequisite of the
caller-location chain. `repr(transparent)`, PhantomData's Pointee role and
NonNull's diagnostic role remain, then `Location`, the caller ABI and
`PanicInfo`/`panic_impl`. No client can build a slice value until
`CoerceUnsized` and unsizing coercions transport.

Lane C [const inherent methods](parallel/const-inherent-methods.md) is joined
on main. Version 13 carries const methods of inherent impls,
including impls that capture lifetimes as core's `impl<'a> Arguments<'a>` does,
with no new tag or shape. Each method uses the existing const constness and
const-definition context. Validation re-proves the body as it re-proves a free
const original, and every consumer re-checks and evaluates it with the
captured regions as leading early parameters. No const evidence travels: a
forged const header over a body that calls an ordinary function is rejected by
name. Version 13 also reads a field of a call's temporary (`make().n`), which
main rejected even in ordinary functions. Writing through such a field stays
fenced, a recorded deviation, since rustc 1.98.0 admits it. Ten clients run
natively and exit 42. `const unsafe`, early-lifetime and trait-impl const
methods stay fenced by name. A static context stays blocked: core's `Sync` is
an unsafe auto trait that does not export. Separately, main accepts a
source-client static without a `sync` lang item, which rustc rejects. Lane C's
next task, another provider's region nominals in a signature, is blocked on
multi-packet source export: no exported row names another artifact, and a
source core cannot import another packet. The caller-location ABI is blocked on
core's real `Location` fields: raw-pointer type rows, `repr(transparent)`,
PhantomData's Pointee role and NonNull's diagnostic role. The `panic_impl`
extern and unsizing fence are blocked on the same chain plus slice rows,
`PanicInfo` and `CoerceUnsized`.

Lane C [tuple and unit struct constructors](parallel/struct-constructors-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries a tuple or unit
struct's constructor with no new tag or shape. It is the struct's only kind-11
variant declaration, bound in the struct's module value namespace, and tuple
fields are named by their ordinals. It extends Lane B's variant-binding
dispatch, whose struct-parent branch now binds the constructor. A fresh
consumer re-derives the constructor's shape, payload, name and visibility from the struct. The
visibility is the narrowest of the struct's and its fields', as rustc computes
it. Consumers call, read, destructure and match imported constructors and
reject narrowed ones as rustc does (E0603, E0616, E0451). Eleven clients run
natively and exit 42. Older profiles keep main's gate by name. `struct T();`,
`#[non_exhaustive]` tuple and unit structs and the `phantom_data` role stay
fenced by name, so core's `PhantomData` is still blocked on the Pointee
contract.

Lane A [census batch 8](parallel/census-batch8.md) repairs census 8's crate
record and the one after it. Census 9 (`10c45c62`, from `97110f230` on main
`4a9a3441f`) checks 42,310 declarations, with none failed or blocked. Its one
crate record is "invalid canonical trait selection" at `option.rs:1386`.
Bodies are not entered, so Milestone 2 is not reached.

- Sizing follows rustc 1.98's hierarchy. A rigid projection is `MetaSized` or
  `Sized` from its associated type's own item bounds, even when an
  environment sizing assumption over another projection cannot be compared
  with it, and `PointeeSized` holds for every type. This repairs core's `Pin`
  PartialEq impl (`pin.rs:1105`) and `Flatten` Iterator impl
  (`iter/adapters/flatten.rs:226`).
  - 13 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle,
    and all agree.
  - The only `sema::traits` changes are in `selection/search/inputs.rs` and
    `selection/search/sizing_fallback.rs`.
- `reached_intrinsics_cannot_execute_their_authored_fallback` expects
  `ctpop`'s constant handler: for `ctpop(7)`, `[u8; 3]` is accepted and
  `[u8; 7]` rejected. `bswap`, which has no handler, keeps the original
  rejection.
- Every PA1 to PA24 suite passes, with PA20 at 1451/1451.
- What remains:
  - `option.rs:1386`: `Option<&'a T::Target>` under `T: [const] Deref` fails
    with "selected type retains an unmapped lifetime" in nominal conjunction
    selection. Lane A owns it next, because Lane B is repairing the
    nightly private failures.
  - Keep-going across the crate-wide declaration passes is not added. It
    needs a dependency audit of about 29 passes that publish facts later
    passes read. A labelled, analysis-only lookahead is approved instead.

Lane B [scoped body proofs, variant re-exports and `--extern
core=`](parallel/scoped-proofs-shim-globs.md) clears three of its assigned
items.

- A generic body that selects an impl with a quantified condition grafts that
  selection's evidence below its query's universe and keeps foreign intervals
  excluded. This fixes "invalid scoped proof evidence".
  - 17 PA31 controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle; all agree.
  - Two caller-lifetime gaps remain, with a separate cause that main shares.
- Core's `prelude::v1` variant re-exports travel as value-namespace module
  bindings to the variant's constructor declaration. The consumer re-derives
  each one from its installed enum parent and rejects a struct parent by
  name.
- `--extern core=` may name a crate's direct `core` dependency, and the
  `no_std` injection resolves through it.
- 9 PA29 cases were declared before the oracle; all agree, natively where
  they run.
- Decided by the integrator and not yet implemented:
  - the shim's `pub use core::*` as a tag-14 version-5 `GLOB` row;
  - `#![compiler_builtins]` as a crate-loader flags record in the legacy
    profile chain.

Lane A [census batch 7](parallel/census-batch7.md) gives `intrinsics::assume` a
constant-evaluation handler and repairs the first four crate-wide declaration
records. Census 8 (`f99b369d`, from `9f04f8694` on main `519f8eda3`) checks
42,310 declarations, with none failed or blocked (census 7: 42,280, 5 and 25).
Its one crate record is "trait argument does not satisfy its declaration
requirements" at `pin.rs:1105`. Bodies are not entered, so Milestone 2 is not
reached.

- A validated source `assume` returns unit for a true condition and fails
  constant evaluation for a false one, as rustc's E0080 does. Its fallback
  body is never entered.
- With Lane B's last sites, the per-definition loop has no failure, and four
  crate records followed in turn, each repaired:
  - a source-library declaration-time body that relates instances of generic
    lifetime nominals (`fmt::Arguments<'a>` from `pat.rs`'s `panic!`) demands
    their reached variances and outlives contracts, waiting once on a queued
    field template;
  - a recursively const-checked body may call an intrinsic whose source
    fallback body is itself safe to expose (`cold_path` from
    `intrinsics::unlikely`), as in rustc 1.98;
  - Drop coverage proves binder-scoped bounds such as `F: [const] FnOnce(T)`
    inside their scope (`mem/drop_guard.rs`);
  - a pattern type is `[const] Destruct` as its base type is (`array::Drain`'s
    `NonNull<T>` field).
- 33 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle, and
  all agree, after one recorded fixture amendment. Every PA1 to PA24 suite
  passes, with PA20 at 1451/1451.
- What remains:
  - `pin.rs:1105`: rigid projections lack the implicit `MetaSized` of a
    `?Sized` associated type, and `PointeeSized`, in trait selection. This is
    shared trait selection work, and Lane A owns it next.
  - Ordinary crates keep the old order for early lifetime contracts. Extending
    the demand renumbers 11 PA20 references.
  - `later-projected-copy-wait`, `proj-c` and `residual-try-type-mismatch` are
    unchanged.

Lane C [methods of inherent impls that capture lifetimes](parallel/impl-region-methods-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries methods of
`impl<'a> X<'a>` with no new tag or shape. The impl's lifetimes lead each
method's original and public frames. A call's application carries them as
leading region arguments and opens only the method's own late slots. A fresh
consumer rebuilds each method template with the captures as early
parameters and re-proves every declared captured and late result. It rejects
escapes, invariant shrinking, E0515 and E0597 cases, as rustc does. Thirteen
clients run natively and exit 42. Trait impls that capture lifetimes, early
or bounded method lifetimes and const inherent methods stay fenced by name.
Core's `Arguments::from_str` is a const inherent method of
`impl<'a> Arguments<'a>`, so the const fence is on the panic path. A second
checkpoint, [writes through unique references](parallel/unique-writes-transport.md),
fixes gap 6 under version 13: a write or mutable reborrow through a `&mut`
field of an immutable by-value binding is admitted as rustc admits it, and
older profiles keep main's gate by name. Six clients run natively.

Lane B [override bounds, region-binding sugar and impl header
projections](parallel/core-sites-reexports.md) clears the last four "associated
type requires an unambiguous implementation" sites (`iter/mod.rs:376`,
`scan.rs:60`, `map_windows.rs:245`, `control_flow.rs:427`). Lane B's census on
base `16c57acb8` (pin `330ee2f8`) checks 42,280 declarations: 4 failed and 26
blocked. On base `0311a54ea` (pin `e9cc215f`) it checked 42,274, with 8 and 28.

- An override's bounds are compared with the trait method's in the impl's
  normal forms, so a repeated `Self::Item` bound is the same requirement.
- An impl parameter that only a region-binding callable bound's `Output`
  determines is inferred from one instance at fresh regions, as rustc's
  placeholder instantiation does.
- An impl header that names a projection of its own parameter is normalized
  under the impl's bounds.
- 26 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle, and
  all agree. One fixture amendment came before oracle v2. Three gaps are
  recorded: two generic bodies that select a region-binding impl, and an impl
  with `where Self:` beside a header projection.
- Diagnosed, then decided by the integrator
  ([plan](core-census-plan.md)) and assigned to Lane B:
  - `--extern core=` through the `rustc_std_workspace_core` shim, as one
    version-13 glob row naming the foreign module, which the consumer
    re-resolves;
  - variant re-exports (`prelude::v1`) as a value-namespace
    `ModuleBindingRow` to a `Variant` declaration under a version-13 gate;
  - `compiler_builtins` injection with a transported `#![compiler_builtins]`
    flag and a RustGM-built stand-in for the PA29 no_std clients.

Lane A [census batch 6](parallel/census-batch6.md) clears both declaration
causes outside Lane B's that census 6 left. Census 7 (`a650f86d`, from
`38edd1bf3` on main `0311a54ea`) checks 42,280 declarations, with 5 failed and
25 blocked (census 6: 42,226, 32 and 52).

- "Canonical trait selection requires pending declarations" (3 failed, 26
  blocked) is repaired by the chosen option (a). Early scalar validation now
  admits impls whose subject is a nominal applying exactly the impl's own type
  parameters, such as derived `Copy for Option<T>` and `NonZero`'s `Copy`. A
  declaration-time constant whose Copy decision selects a queued impl, field
  template or contract waits for that fact. The wait goes through the
  worklist's existing header dependency edge and cycle detection, and is never
  answered from declaration availability. The only `sema::traits` change is a
  `Hash` derive on `selection::Dependency`.
- `pattern_type!(T is !null)` over a type parameter is accepted, as in core's
  `pat.rs` `DispatchFromDyn` impl, and instances must be raw pointers. Integer
  and reference bases are rejected by name, although rustc 1.98 accepts them.
- 17 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle, and
  all agree. They include one recorded gap and three intentionally stricter
  bases. Every PA1 to PA24 suite passes, with PA20 at 1451/1451.
- What remains:
  - Lane B's cause: 4 failed, 2 blocked.
  - "Source intrinsic constant execution is not implemented": 1 failed, 23
    blocked. The `ilog10` lengths of `fmt/num_buffer.rs` now reach
    `hint::assert_unchecked`, whose `intrinsics::assume` has no CTFE handler.
    This needs a decision.
  - Recorded gaps: a later-declared projected nominal destructured in a const
    fn, `size_of::<Wrap<u8>>()` over a projected field, and
    `residual-try-type-mismatch`'s cause text.

Lane C [lifetime-parameterized structs and enums](parallel/lifetime-nominals-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries a struct's or
enum's lifetimes as leading region slots, which its fields name in its own
frame. Its Self recipe, applications and tuple-variant constructor calls
carry region arguments first. No variance or outlives claim travels: a fresh
consumer derives both from the imported fields, accepts covariant shrinking
and rejects invariant shrinking, lifetime growth, escapes and a missing
implied `T: 'a`, as rustc does. Nine clients run natively and exit 42.
Packets without nominal lifetimes are byte-identical. Declared lifetime
bounds and methods of `impl<'a> X<'a>` are rejected by name. The next blocker
is the captured callable frame of such methods (`Arguments::new_const`,
`Location::caller`, `PanicInfo::new`).

Lane B [specialization selection, user callable outputs and no_std source
clients](parallel/specialization-nostd.md) clears 19 of the 23 failed
"associated type requires an unambiguous implementation" sites. Lane B's census
on base `67c01c493` (pin `bab534e4`) checks 42,250 declarations: 31 failed and
30 blocked. Lane B's previous census had 42,122, 62 and 126. That cause falls
from 23/25 to 4/1.

- Selection follows rustc's `min_specialization` winnowing. Before the graph
  closes, one pairwise comparison drops a candidate that another applicable
  candidate specializes. An omitted associated type comes from the nearest
  defining ancestor. All 14 `StepBy`, `Fuse` and `Zip` sites pass. A child
  that overrides a final parent type is rejected by name.
- A user callable-trait impl for a struct, or for `&mut X` whose referent is
  not callable, is selected beside language evidence. This clears five sites
  in `SyncView` and `array::Drain`.
- `#![no_std]` source clients over a provider named `core` get rustc's
  injected `core` extern-prelude entry and their edition's
  `core::prelude::rust_20xx` glob. Rust 2021 clients pass the same gate.
  Thirteen declared controls match rustc natively.
- Still open:
  - region-binding sugar (`scan.rs`, `map_windows.rs`);
  - override conformance of a repeated `Self::Item` where-clause
    (`iter/mod.rs:376`), a `sema::traits` fix assigned to Lane B;
  - `control_flow.rs:427`;
  - variant re-exports in a source-core export, which original core's
    `prelude::v1` uses, assigned to Lane B;
  - `--extern core=` naming the direct dependency, as rustc does. std's
    graph passes the `rustc_std_workspace_core` shim as `core` to six units.

Lane A [census batch 5](parallel/census-batch5.md) repairs six of census 5's
causes and two that those repairs exposed. Census 6 (`1c0cac9f`) checks 42,226
declarations, with 32 failed and 52 blocked (census 5: 42,194, 62 and 55).

- "Invalid canonical trait selection" (18) and "invalid associated equality
  selection" (3) had one cause. In a crate with trait objects, equality
  selection asked a rigid projection for its constructor head and returned the
  normalization error. That error now means "not an object". This is one match
  in `sema/traits/results.rs`, the batch's only `sema::traits` change. Five
  `flatten.rs` methods now reach Lane B's cause.
- Declaration-time constants:
  - early scalar impl validation prepares the value shapes it will form, such
    as `NonZeroU8Inner`, which is declared later;
  - implicit enum discriminants are published with the enum's shape;
  - const bodies and constant execution wait for a later struct's field
    template instead of failing;
  - source `ctpop`, `ctlz`, `cttz` and their `_nonzero` forms execute.
- In census mode, a use of a failed impl header's self type is blocked by that
  header's record. So is a const body that a failed root left active. Before,
  they failed again as "later semantic assignments" and as a cycle.
- As in rustc 1.98, item-signature anonymous constants skip semantic type
  privacy. Body constants and associated item privacy stay checked; `spec.md`
  records the rule.
- 27 PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle, and
  all agree. Every PA1 to PA24 suite passes, with PA20 at 1451/1451.
- What remains:
  - Lane B's cause: 28 failed, 26 blocked.
  - "Canonical trait selection requires pending declarations": 3 failed, 26
    blocked. It holds the largest roots, two `Buf`s and `prepare`, and needs a
    decision. Their CTFE `Copy` of `Option<T>` and `NonZero<T>` waits for impls
    that early validation does not admit.
  - `pat.rs`'s generic `!null` pattern type (1) is diagnosed only.

Lane C [cold, caller-tracking and `panic` role rows](parallel/panic-attributes-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries two new bits in a
function header row's inline byte (`#[cold]`, `#[track_caller]`) and a
spelled `panic` role row. Rows without the bits keep their exact bytes. A
fresh consumer re-proves each tracking flag against its own installed header
and the role's `fn(&'static str) -> !` signature. It runs a provider with
core 1.98's `panic` attributes and role natively. Calls to tracked functions
stay fenced by name until `Location` and the caller ABI transport. Five named
forgeries and every strict prefix are rejected, and one cleared flag is
accepted as sound. The next blocker is lifetime-parameterized nominals
(`Arguments<'a>`, `Location<'a>`, `PanicInfo<'a>`), which stop at "source
lifetime binding has no actual original slot". PA20 passes 1,451/1,451.

Lane B [definition side table, lineage ledger and sugar-determined impl
parameters](parallel/definition-lineage.md) repairs the expansion `Definition`
regression and the census's largest cause. It also brings in std's two profile
decisions.

- The expansion `Definition` is 136 bytes again. Imported macro identities and
  images live in their bound rows, which install first, so definition `i` below
  the installed count is row `i`. Every exported macro table is byte-identical.
- The import-lineage ledger is granted per admitted source byte (8 steps,
  128 attempted bytes) above its former fixed floor, like the lint ledger, and
  shares its work record.
- A callable-sugar clause whose binder binds no region is now an ordinary impl
  condition, so `T` in core's `impl<T, F> Iterator for FromFn<F> where
  F: FnMut() -> Option<T>` resolves. Lane B's own census on base `e84a85783`
  (pin `4cb211bb`, a separate run from Lane A's census 5) checks 42,122
  declarations: 62 failed and 126 blocked, down from census 4's 74 and 127. "Associated type requires an
  unambiguous implementation" falls from 35/26 to 23/25, with no new failure.
  Specialization (14), user callable-trait `Self::Output` (5) and
  region-binding sugar (2) remain. A newly exposed gap is override conformance
  of a repeated `Self::Assoc` where-clause, which fails without sugar too.
- Std builds as an rlib only: a `dylib` beside `rlib`/`lib` is dropped and
  recorded per unit; dylib-only, cdylib-only and staticlib-only targets stay
  typed gaps.
- 2015 and 2018 source clients are admitted, and a pre-2021 `unreachable!`
  delegates to `unreachable_2015`. Those are the only legacy rules cfg-if and
  rustc-demangle need beyond the ones already implemented. Pre-2021 `panic!` and
  `assert!` stay rejected by name.

Lane A [census batch 4](parallel/census-batch4.md) repairs census 4's largest
blocking cause and its roots. Census 5 (`5cb7e00c`) checks 42,194
declarations, with 62 failed and 55 blocked (census 4: 42,109, 74 and 127).

- "missing storage opaque declaration" (4 failed, 101 blocked) was confirmed
  by an analysis-only diagnostic. An opaque result is interned before its
  declaration, and storage preparation for a later `size_of` length met it and
  failed the unrelated item. Such an application now waits on its own identity.
  An ordinary crate whose opaque waits for a GAT const argument is accepted.
- Its eight roots, `impl Trait + '_` results ("outlives bound requires a named
  lifetime"), read `'_` as the elided output lifetime, as rustc does. A
  where-clause `T: '_` stays rejected (E0637).
- `repr_bitpacked`'s four "semantic type or associated item is private": a
  binderless const or static annotation now types its constants in its own
  module, not the crate root.
- The declaration own-bound check groups assumptions by their first closed
  value. The 256-bound budget family is linear (2 steps per bound at 64, 256 and
  1,024), and a genuinely ambiguous opponent is still rejected. Batch 3's
  160-to-192 regime was an attribution-probe dump artifact; the pin's cap was
  at about 250 bounds.
- Thirteen PA31 no_core controls were declared before the rustc 1.98.0 oracle
  (all agree). Every PA1 to PA24 suite passes, and PA20 is 1451/1451.
- Census into bodies was not implemented: about 30 later declaration passes
  are not isolated per item, and the second one stops on a census-poisoned
  declaration. Four causes the cascade hid now appear. They hold the two
  largest blocking roots (`nonzero.rs`, 12; `int_log10.rs`, 11), plus `ctpop`
  const execution and an enum-discriminant layout. "invalid canonical trait
  selection" (18) is still unreduced.

Lane C [`&'static str`, `!` and static text](parallel/static-str-never-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries:

- `str` and `!` type rows (recipe tags 10 and 11);
- static text literals (value node 7), with a text column present exactly
  when a body has a literal;
- plain reborrow inputs, so `&x` arguments and merged literals cross;
- SSA snapshots in branching bodies, when each use stays in its block.

A fresh consumer now runs the shapes of core's `panicking::panic(&'static
str) -> !` and generic `assert_failed` natively. Lane B's alloc census
counts 120 and 22 sites behind these two. The consumer checks each literal's
type and bytes and interns it itself. Six named forgeries and every strict
prefix are rejected, and a rewritten literal is accepted as another sound
program. Core's own `panic` still needs `#[track_caller]`, `#[cold]`,
`#[lang = "panic"]` and the fmt roles. PA20 passes 1,451/1,451.

Lane C [provider-body guards and single-variant matches](parallel/provider-guards-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries match guards in
provider bodies:

- guard view locals (local kind 3);
- guard start, use, view-binding and select actions (nodes 2, 3, 4 and 6);
- the imaginary edge (terminator 1);
- guard columns after the calls, present exactly when a body starts a guard.

Fresh consumers run their own guard ownership proof and require a live
constructor-test fact at each bound view, following only real edges. A
single-variant `match` or `let` now crosses: local lowering and the consumer
share one rule, under which a variant with no sibling needs no test. Six
named forgeries and every strict prefix are rejected; two sound relabels are
accepted. Packets without guards keep their exact bytes, and PA20 passes
1,451/1,451.

Lane B [imported panic family and semiopaque rows](parallel/macro-metadata.md#imported-panic-family-and-semiopaque-rows-version-4)
adds tag-14 version 4. A fresh 2024 consumer now runs an imported `panic!`,
`unreachable!`, `assert!` and the wrappers around them. Each delegates to its
provider's semiopaque `panic_2021`/`unreachable_2021` row through
`$crate`, bound by crate identity; the native client prints rustc's
`41 43 44 41`.

- The edition-panic contract is the `edition_panic` member of a row's
  allowance list, so it adds no bytes; `allow_internal_unsafe` is one flags
  byte per definition row, granted only to that row's own frame.
- Semiopaque `pub macro` rows are kind 3. The single-rule `macro m(...)` form
  (core's `const_error`) and transparent rows are rejected by name.
- An uncalled single `use` of an unstable row now needs its feature, as pinned
  rustc requires (E0658); a glob import does not.
- Two private invariants on main are repaired first: the hot `Imported` record
  is back to 160 bytes (macro rows share one cold box with original
  reservation work), and the const-allowance transport opponent
  `consumer-grant-not-request` again fails at its feature gate (below).
- Builtin derive output now roots its `crate::` paths at the defining crate's
  identity and charges the macro-context ledger, with every `--pa` suite
  unchanged. Cross-crate derive still stops at the imported-builtin gate and
  needs Lane C's trait and impl transport.

Imported `format_args!` still stops by name: its expander and the typed
constructors it emits need Lane C's transport of core's fmt language roles and
constructors first. The fix for `consumer-grant-not-request` corrects a header
ledger that the imported-contract ingest created with an empty input-graph
bound (since `d2bcb1f89`). Still open in this lane's area: the expansion
`Definition` is 168 bytes, not the 136 that eight private invariants assert
(since `cbef4d469`).

The [alloc census by blocker](parallel/macro-metadata.md#census-by-blocker-after-version-4-task-4)
now finds 163 of 337 library macro sites with no macro-level blocker. With
version 3 there were 41: the earlier table's 48 included seven
`debug_assert_eq!` message sites that expand `format_args!`. The default-message
`assert!`, `debug_assert!` and `unreachable!()` sites are the bulk of the new
ones. Of the rest, 60 wait on imported `format_args!`, 113 on cross-crate
derive and one on `const_error!`. The 163 still need Lane C item transport,
chiefly `panicking::panic(&'static str) -> !` (120 sites) and
`panicking::assert_failed` (22).

[Census batch 3](parallel/census-batch3.md) bounds the source lint-policy
ledger by its input. It keeps the former fixed limits as a floor, and adds
8 steps and 64 attempted bytes per admitted original source byte. Each grant is
made before the producer's first charge, and a failed ledger stays failed.
Core uses 0.69 steps and 3.65 bytes per byte. The batch also applies rustc's
builtin scalar operand rule before operator impl selection. Arithmetic,
bitwise, shift, comparison and compound operators then unify integer and float
operands, or relate the result, as rustc does, so `{ $size * $elems }` and
`($n - 1)` no longer face two candidate impls. Unary operand classification
leaves definition waits to the body loop, which fixes `static-length-neg-sync`.
Twelve new PA31 controls agree with rustc 1.98.0. One existing control is
amended: `source-shift-or/readiness/shared.rs`, recorded as an unresolved
frontier, is now accepted, as rustc accepts it. The 256-bound "type constraint
work budget exceeded" is a quadratic own-bound ambiguity scan in `choose`, not
linear work. It is left unchanged, with a repair proposed.

Census 4 (analysis-only, pin `6e53028c`, inputs unchanged, 47 s, 375 opens) is
the first to complete the declaration loop. It checked 42,109 declarations:
74 failed and 127 blocked (census 3: 92 and 622). It ended at "stopped
declarations", not at a budget, and bodies were not reached. The `any.rs`
"object requires exactly one principal trait" failures are gone. They predated
`b9af97afd` (source auto-trait object markers), which census 3's pin lacked.
The ordinary first error is now cause 4 (`pat.rs`, non-pointer `!null`).
The largest blocker is "missing storage opaque declaration" (4 failed, 101
blocked, behind `to_be_bytes` and the integer `Buf` aliases), with a
hypothesis recorded but not yet probed. The most frequent failure is
"associated type requires an unambiguous implementation" (35). Evidence:
`rustgm-pa29-work/obj/parallel/core-census-4/`.

Lane C [inherent methods and late-region originals](parallel/inherent-methods-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 carries:

- inherent impls on provider structs and enums, as the absent-trait impl
  header and member links, with no new wire tag;
- free originals with late region formals, including named ones.

Consumers publish imported methods in their nominal's inherent bucket. Their
own lookup, auto-ref and path calls select those methods, and each call is an
ordinary application. Consumers also re-prove each original's body result
against its declared result. A forged `-> &'b u8` was accepted before this
change and is now rejected. The frozen `Columns` record is back to 768 bytes:
the version-13 cold groups moved out of line.

[Census batch 2](parallel/census-batch2.md) prepares impl header facts on demand
through the declaration worklist, with cycle detection:

- operator method headers and `Output` contracts behind array lengths and
  initializers, for binary and unary operators;
- associated result contracts behind impl where-clauses;
- an impl's own trait reference behind `Self::` shorthand.

Failed census headers stay item-local, and their members are blocked rather
than failed. The 16 "invalid or exhausted" messages are split. Thirteen
declared PA31/PA20 controls agree with rustc 1.98.0. Cause 4 (non-pointer
`!null`) stays explicit.

The batch also fixes a LIB-193 regression in four PA20 `assoc-bounds` cases.
In ordinary crates, projected-bound staging charges the header ledger at about
280 bytes per bound-syntax element, linearly, against a factor of 128. The
input bound now counts 4,096 attempted bytes per written `Bound` or associated
`Constraint` node in ordinary crates only. PA20 passes 1,451/1,451.

Census 3 (analysis-only, pin `e29c6ab1`, inputs unchanged, 30 s/375 opens)
checked 37,780 declarations: 92 failed and 622 blocked; bodies were not
reached. It ends at the fixed lint-policy work cap ("source lint policy work
budget exceeded", `ops/arith.rs`). The dominant blocker is rustc's builtin
integer binop rule: `{ $size * $elems }` in `to_bytes.rs` and `($n - 1)` in
`array_impl_default` block 478 items. The ordinary first error is still
`any.rs` (Lane B). Evidence: `rustgm-pa29-work/obj/parallel/core-census-3b/`.

[Macro stability rows and imported allowance ledgers](parallel/macro-metadata.md#stability-rows-and-imported-allowance-ledgers-version-3)
(Lane B, PA29): tag-14 version 3 carries each definition row's `#[unstable]`
feature and each builtin row's `allow_internal_unstable` list. Every call of
an imported unstable row needs the feature, either requested by the crate or
granted by the enclosing imported expansion's list (E0658 otherwise). Source
clients and staged consumers now own the imported-allowance ledger, so listed
rows such as `cfg(ub_checks)` wrappers work there. Uses of unstable imported
APIs inside expansions are admitted by the frame's list, as rustc does. Builtins with
`allow_internal_unsafe` or edition-panic contracts are now rejected at
publication instead of losing their contract. All fourteen declared rustc
outcomes held, and the candidate passes all 58 macro cases natively. Imported `assert`,
`panic`, `unreachable` and `format_args` still do not run. Their next blockers
are `&'static str`/`!` signature transport for `$crate::panicking::panic`,
semiopaque `pub macro` rows for `panic_2021`/`unreachable_2021`, and typed
format constructors.

[Macro re-export rows](parallel/macro-metadata.md#re-export-rows) (Lane B,
PA29): every public macro binding of a crate's public modules is now published.
`pub use` of a macro, including globs and core-shaped preludes, writes a
tag-14 version-2 re-export row (kind 2) naming the defining row by owner
ordinal and key; private-module definitions relocate to their least public
key. Consumers bind re-exports to the definition's `DefId` by crate identity,
never by name, so globs of one macro do not conflict and `#[macro_use]` enters
root re-exports as rustc does. Tables without re-exports keep their exact
bytes. All 15 declared rustc outcomes held; the candidate passes all 44 macro
cases natively. `reexport-provider` is amended from a rejected requirement row
to version 2. Still rejected by name: bindings reachable only through a module
alias, macros 2.0 and context-dependent imported builtins. No_core source
clients do not reach the extern prelude from nested modules (found while
qualifying; unrelated to macros).

Lane C [called constructors and imported variants](parallel/enum-methods-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 now carries called
tuple-variant constructors (`Opt::Some(f(x))`, `Pair::Two(a, b)`) as ordinary
function applications that name the variant, with no new wire tag. Validators
read the enum's formals, payload and Self, and require a safe call with no
binder or method. Consumers register each imported constructor's template,
bind each imported variant in its enum's member scope, and synthesize every
referenced constructor body. Consumer constructor calls, unit paths, patterns,
guards, `match &o` and reference parameters over imported enums return 42
natively, as rustc does. Older exports and all 420 existing fixture outputs
are byte-identical. The enum-match `called` control is amended. Still open
toward `Option::map`: inherent enum impls (`self`/`&self`), late-region
reference parameters and guards in provider bodies, and const callable bounds.

[Builtin macro rows and allowance lists](parallel/macro-metadata.md#builtin-rows-and-allowance-lists-version-2)
(Lane B, PA29): tag-14 version 2 adds builtin rows and per-row
`allow_internal_unstable` lists. Each builtin row carries a name, syntax,
expander identity, kind and module placement, and no image. Crates whose exports
are only plain `macro_rules!` rows still write their exact version-1 bytes.
Consumers bind each placement through the provider's own module identity and
bind their own expander of each identity. Unknown or relabelled identities fail
by name, and only context-free expanders execute. The imported allowance lists
apply exactly where a local definition's permissions do, charged per feature.
All 14 declared rustc outcomes held. The candidate passes all 29 macro cases
natively. Three requirement rows stay rejected by name: an imported `test`
attribute, macros 2.0 exports and macro re-exports. The PA34 mixed rows now
record their metadata outcomes as amended. Census of core macros used by alloc
library code: 125 sites need re-export rows (prelude derives, `derive`,
`core::bstr`), and 161 need executable non-context-free builtins. Only one site
needs macros 2.0 hygiene. Re-export rows are the proposed next step.

Lane C [enum-and-match transport](parallel/enum-match-transport.md) is
joined on main. Version 13 now carries enum and variant
declarations, enum header columns (representation tag, variant shapes,
discriminants and payload fields), constructor tests, variant aggregates,
downcast fields and fieldless enum casts. Fresh consumers re-derive each
enum's layout and nullable policy, re-check discriminant range and
uniqueness, and re-prove every downcast and match exhaustiveness over their
own rebuilt CFG. Fieldless, `#[repr(u8)]`, generic `Opt<T>`, `FnOnce` `map`
and by-reference binding clients return 42 natively, as rustc does. Older
exports are byte-identical. Still open toward `Option::map`: called variant
constructors, inherent enum methods, const callable bounds, guards and
reference scrutinees.

[Macro metadata consumer](parallel/macro-metadata.md#consumer-import) (Lane B,
PA29): clients now import exported `macro_rules!` rows. Each image is lexed
once, its rows are rebuilt and must be equal, and it is compiled in the
transported edition with its rule count checked. Each image gets an empty image
unit. `$crate` resolves through the dependency-indexed crate id to the
provider's identity. `use`, path and `#[macro_use] extern crate` forms are
supported, and later macro-use crates shadow earlier ones. All 15 frozen PA29
cases match rustc (`scripts/check_macro_metadata.py`), and the 7 binaries match
natively. All six PA34 mixed-edition rows now match through metadata, including
original cfg-if 1.0.4 into both consumers. The census of core's exported macros
finds 58 builtin rows of 102, so the proposed next profile row is a builtin
identity with no image.

[Jointness-preserving macro image](parallel/macro-metadata.md#jointness-preserving-image)
(Lane B, PA29): the tag-14 image now keeps source adjacency. Pieces are adjacent
exactly when they were in the definition, and are otherwise separated by one
space. Lexing it again rebuilds the same image and rows. Pinned rustc froze a
cross-crate `stringify!` control in `pa29/tests/macro-spacing-outcomes.json`.
The first prediction (punctuation jointness survives transcription) was refuted:
transcribed tokens print alone, while paren and brace delimiters keep their
source spacing, and a space always follows a bracket group. RustGM's local
printer now spaces after a transcribed `]` as well, and its local copy passes
natively.

[Source-prelude validation once per phase](parallel/macro-budget.md#source-prelude-validation-once-per-phase)
(Lane B): the retained source-prelude selector is now re-authenticated once per
phase (selection, end of expansion, end of resolution, each sema method index),
not on every lookup. Each lookup still pays one work unit and rechecks its
selected module row. On unchanged core the ledger's work fell from 8,398,273 to
6,913,407 (prelude lookups: 1,619,907 to 135,041), with byte-identical census
output and the same `flatten.rs` 8286 frontier. The grant factors are unchanged;
work headroom grows from 3.5× to 4.2×.

[Exported macro metadata](parallel/macro-metadata.md) (Lane B, PA29): library
metadata now publishes root `#[macro_export]` definitions in a separate
version-one envelope (tag 14), nested inside native links. Each row carries a
canonical body image, typed token-tree rows, the edition, `local_inner_macros`
and the rule count. Crates without exported macros keep their exact bytes.
Malformed, relabelled, nested and trailing rows are rejected by name, as are
generated, documented, builtin, scoped and permissioned definitions. Original
cfg-if 1.0.4 now exports its `cfg_if` row (edition 2018, 4 rules). Twelve pinned
rustc outcomes are frozen in `pa29/tests/macro-metadata-outcomes.json`. Consumer
import is still to do: authentication, `$crate` bound to the provider's identity,
the `use`, path and `#[macro_use]` forms, native runs and the cfg-if unit.

Lane C [callable-role transport](parallel/callable-role-transport.md) is
joined on main. Version 13 now carries a no-core `Tuple`
marker, `FnOnce` with its declared `Output`, `FnMut` and `Fn`, together with
tuple recipes, declared-result bindings, `Rvalue::Invoke` call operators and
two-phase receiver borrows. Fresh consumers re-authenticate every role header
and call method, and re-prove each call operator's trait and `Output` in
their own rebuilt environment. Function-item, own-closure and own-capturing
clients of `apply`, `apply_mut` and `apply_ref` return 42 natively, as rustc
does. Older exports are byte-identical. Still open: enums with match,
closures inside provider bodies, and consumer-written callable bounds.

[Input-proportional macro budget](parallel/macro-budget.md) (Lane B): the
resolve-phase macro-contract and inventory ledger is no longer capped by a fixed
whole-crate 1M/64 MiB default. It starts from that setup floor. Then the first
parse of each authenticated source identity grants 2 work units, 64 attempted
bytes and 8 retained bytes per original byte. Expansion output and copies grant
nothing. Explicit `--source-contract-*` values cap the allowance, and failure
stays sticky. Attribution on unchanged core measured 0.573 work, 16.7 attempted
bytes and a 1.88-byte retained peak per admitted byte (14.67 MB), all from
linear payers. Under default limits the core target unit now passes `ip_addr.rs`.
Its new first gap is `flatten.rs` 8286, "missing associated result contract",
the same frontier and byte-identical census as under the observer's explicit
caps. With Cargo's lint policy, core still stops first on the
`implicit_provenance_casts` producer.

The expansion `Definition` regression (168 bytes against the 136 that eight
private layout invariants assert) is repaired by Lane B's
[definition side table](parallel/definition-lineage.md). The per-crate `Report`
assertion reads 3104 and the `Engine` 4152, 8 bytes each for the lineage
ledger's grant word. The `Imported` (160
bytes) and `consumer-grant-not-request` regressions are repaired in
`d77e1c445` and `139078dbb`.

Lane C [declared associated-result transport](parallel/associated-result-transport.md)
is joined on main. Version 13 now carries trait associated results, impl
definitions and projections as header columns. These are present only when a
kind-9 declaration exists, so older packets keep their bytes. Fresh consumers
re-prove each definition, check impl methods against it and normalize every
projection themselves. Imported `Add`/`Sub`/`Mul` roles with `Output` are
admitted. Generic `<T as Shape>::Item` and `<T as Add>::Output` bodies return 42
natively, and the overflow client aborts, as rustc does. The language-roles
`Output` boundary is amended. A consumer's own `+=` and `+` now resolve through
the imported role's validated identity (native 42), amending the
`own-operator` boundary. Still open: the `Fn*` roles (tuple recipes,
rust-call), enums with match and closures.

[Legacy editions](parallel/legacy-editions.md) (Lane B): the CLI and the target
unit executor accept Rust 2015 and 2018. Pinned rustc froze 100 outcomes, 82 of
which are declared RustGM expectations. Two rules are implemented: the pre-2021
`pat` fragment and whole-variable closure captures. So are the 2015 crate-root
`use`/`::` paths and the injected root crate. Other differences fail with a typed
fence. The original cfg-if 1.0.4 passes all analysis phases under 2018. Its
library metadata stops on the exported `macro_rules!`, because metadata carries
no macro definitions. That gap is edition-independent. Original rustc-demangle
under 2015 stops on the bootstrap library model (`core::char`, `unreachable!`).
No_core negative literal patterns now require the `neg` item and `T: Neg`, as
rustc does (PA31 `source-negative-patterns`).

Lane C [language-role transport](parallel/language-role-transport.md) is joined
on main. Version 13 now carries spelled
language roles, trait `Self` defaults and checked Add/Sub/Mul rows. Imported
`AddAssign`, `SubAssign` and `MulAssign` re-authenticate in fresh consumers.
Genuine source `+=`, `-=` and `*=` transport as checked Binary rows, and each
consumer lowers its own overflow guard. In-range clients exit 42 natively and
overflow clients abort, as rustc does. Older exports are
byte-identical. Still open: a consumer's own operator use, `Output` with the
version-11 fold, the `Fn*` roles, enums with match, and closures.

[Census batch 1](parallel/census-batch1.md) does two things:

- Projection-premise failures now report their real cause. Genuine exhaustion
  is worded separately and stays sticky.
- An impl parameter can be determined through the output of a quantified
  `Fn(..) -> T` clause, exactly when its inputs are determined. Twelve PA24/PA21
  controls agree with rustc.

The second analysis-only census (CLI `e6f4a7ec`, 22.0 s/375 opens, inputs
unchanged) checked 10,408 declarations: 6 failed, 31 blocked, bodies not
reached. The largest cause is `ops/arith.rs` `Sub::sub`. A `[T; (32 - 1)]` impl
header queries that method's attribute header before function headers exist, and
the check misreports the absence as "changed its semantic function". That cause
blocks 31 `array_impl_default!` impls. The other failures:

- `any.rs:162`/`:169` (Lane B);
- `flatten.rs:316`, a missing associated result contract;
- `pin.rs:1745`, `Self::Target` in a trait impl's where-clause;
- `pat.rs:85`, a non-pointer `!null` pattern type.

The census stops at the first-error check `finish_constant_headers` in
`prepare_trait_implementations` ("implementation candidate inventory still has
pending headers"). Evidence: `rustgm-pa29-work/obj/parallel/core-census-2/`.

The first original-core census (analysis-only, joined CLI `87c8e187`, 20.6 s/375
opens, unchanged inputs and limits) checked 1,197 declarations: 7 failed, none
blocked, and bodies were not reached. Five failures are impl parameters
constrained only through `Fn(..) -> T` bounds, which the impl-header constraint
check reads as unscoped quantified clauses (`array/drain.rs`, `iter/adapters/filter_map.rs`).
Two are core's own auto traits counted as a second object principal (`any.rs:162`,
`:169`). The census stopped at `iter/adapters/flatten.rs:316`, whose fixed message
"invalid or exhausted canonical projection premise" hides the inner error and was
classified as a budget stop. Evidence: `rustgm-pa29-work/obj/parallel/core-census-1/`.

Census batch 1 ([auto-trait object principals](parallel/object-auto-principals.md)):
object bounds now classify core's own auto traits using the declaration's
`auto` header and its catalog role, not the name. Pinned-Rust fixtures: 14
inputs, of which 13 match. A custom auto trait beside a principal is the
recorded gap. The analysis-only re-census (candidate CLI `a071a3a3`, 22.2 s)
checked 1,199 declarations with 5 failed. Both `any.rs` records are gone, there
are no new object-related records, and it still stops at `flatten.rs:316`.

The [target unit executor](parallel/target-unit-executor.md) now runs on core,
alloc and std graphs re-planned under RustGM's fixed profile. The dev-profile
graphs are kept as history. One combined run executes the real host build
scripts, which reproduce their frozen outputs, and feeds them to the target
units. No profile gap remains. With main CLI `7051deee`, core fails first under
Cargo's lint policy, where core is uncapped: `strict provenance lint producer is
not implemented` at `lib.rs:3519`, `#![deny(implicit_provenance_casts)]`. With
analysis-only capped lints, core instead stops at `source macro contracts exceed
their work budget` (`net/ip_addr.rs:9035`), under default source-contract limits.
Alloc has no other gap. Std still needs Rust 2018 (cfg_if), Rust 2015
(rustc_demangle) and the std dylib.

The [analysis-only census mode](parallel/core-census-mode.md) is on main:
`--census-output PATH` and observer `--census`. It records every isolated
declaration and body failure, each blocked dependent once, sticky budget stops
and per-phase counts. It always exits 1 and is never a qualification. Its first
original-core census is recorded above.

Source profile `module-branches-v12` is on main: transported originals keep typed
Branch and checked Panic exits, with fresh per-instance proof and native execution
([checkpoint](parallel/body-transport-branches.md)). Earlier profiles keep their
bytes. No source construct emits Panic yet, and match, drops, closures, impl
methods, references and constants remain untransported.

Source profile `module-bodies-v13` is on main: a stable generic typed body IR
node encoding, with closed primitive comparison and bitwise Binary values
([checkpoint](parallel/body-ir-transport.md)). Generic literal, or- and
range-pattern decisions transport and execute natively. Unreconstructable
allocated forms reject by name, and older profiles keep their bytes. A genuine
Panic producer stays blocked by operator-role, parameter-default and
associated-type header transport; enum declarations and closures also remain.
Lane C found that RustGM accepts the negative range pattern `-5..=-1` without a
`neg` language item, which rustc 1.98.0 rejects. PA31 `source-negative-patterns`
now owns and rejects it.

Current qualified LIB-193 observation reports `object requires exactly one
principal trait` at file 97 (original `core/src/any.rs`, byte 6032, the `Send`
bound of `dyn Any + Send` at line 162), in 20.09 s/375 opens. The
[header budget checkpoint](parallel/lib193-header-budget.md) attributes the
crossing to linear producers plus per-site `enable` reparsing (6,292 sites, 85
spellings). It validates each spelling once per collection walk and replaces
the borrowed fixed header caps with a once-computed input-graph bound (16 work
units and 128 attempted bytes per element). Manifest, pins, cap-lints and limits
are unchanged. No original artifact or whole assignment is complete.

The preceding joined original-core observation reported **LIB-193**, the unlocated
source-header query-work guard, in19.6071s/375opens. The
[cold/deprecation integration](parallel/cold-deprecation-main-join.md) clears
LIB192 with genuine staged release semantics and actual prototype owners.
Four private,23 regular and six native controls, strict selected/private Clippy,
format/architecture,282 harness/460 roots and upstream checks pass. All722
original hashes, six current configuration-source pins, options and limits stay
unchanged. A separate Source97574 diagnostic without cold44f0 identifies the last

with storage14,686,030 below16M; aggregate attribution remains unresolved. No
source location or exact combined caller is inferred. User requested handoff and

its isolated unqualified conditional callable WIP. No original artifact or whole
assignment after24 is complete.

The preceding joined original-core observation reported **LIB-192**, staged future or
nonnumeric deprecation policy, at file7/expanded byte2756, in19.1083s/375opens.
The [captured closure/pointer integration](parallel/captured-pointer-main-join.md)
clears LIB191 through the genuine declaration and reached pointer operation,
qualifies actual shared/mutable/owned const execution and component reuse, and

and six native controls pass on the joined compiler. File7 is original
num/shells/legacy_int_modules.rs; generated2756 is beyond its2706 physical bytes
and is not assigned a physical source line. Source owns the actual staged
release policy; Async owns remaining conditional/generic const callable proof,

The preceding joined original-core observation reported **LIB-191**, the actual
`ptr_guaranteed_cmp` declaration's do-not-const-check policy at
core/src/intrinsics/mod.rs:2297 (file47, byte88680), in18.1196s/375opens.
The [source header allocation checkpoint](parallel/source-header-allocation.md)
clears LIB-190 without changing any of the722 inputs, six configuration pins,
profile/options or limits. Its diagnostic proved a local byte failure at23,219
headers: four owner slots per single-owner row plus duplicate header/map charges;
cold-cache preparations and visits were zero. Initial owner buffers now reserve
one slot and only genuinely new rows/bindings pay their map envelope. Real source

No original core/alloc/std artifact or whole PA after24 is complete.

The [complete Field offset/native checkpoint](parallel/field-offset-execution.md)
now executes the actual initializer/intrinsic/layout and full frozen observer
`field 4 23 29 37 41 47 31 1`. Eight private, two native and15 regular/scaling/

Full field_of/reflection/transport and all original artifact gates remain required.

The join passes21 exact public controls, strict all-target metadata,44 explicit
unstable Rust profile observations, all22 unchanged course output triples,
complete PA20 independent checks and282 harness checks. Full ac104 CI passes

The Field/closure/pointer and cold/deprecation handoffs are integrated. Source's

The [Field operation checkpoint](parallel/field-proof-projection-checkpoint.md)

Provided OFFSET selection/initializer/field_offset/layout, field_of, complete

The [automatic-trait main join](parallel/custom-auto-main-join.md) qualifies actual

The [trait Self CI repair](parallel/self-type-ci-repair.md) addresses the67 shared

The [Field/inherent join](parallel/field-headers-main-join.md) qualifies ordinary

The [Reborrow source-owner repair](parallel/reborrow-source-owner-repair.md)

Root owns joined publication and Field's demanded implementation/projection/

## Assignment closure queue

All rows below remain open. This queue identifies the next reconciliation, not
an exhaustive fresh audit of every historical unsupported case. PA25–PA31 have
no published text-test manifest. Preserve complete handout requirements and
independent outcomes; missing release packaging and missing semantics are
different debts. Reconcile existing fixtures at consumer exits instead of
creating another parallel test corpus or repeatedly running every PA.

| Owning PA | Library-critical work first | Closure review at the next consumer exit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| PA25 | Source const operations and imported const/generic calls required by the actual core path | Reconcile constants-view, provenance/promotion and sequence outcomes; identify remaining valid unsupported inputs, then review the text vocabulary and references. |
| PA26 | Source-owned operators/desugaring and actual panic/format constructors | Reconcile iteration, question-mark, derive and formatting contracts against source-library execution. Do not count a bootstrap factory as compiling its upstream implementation. |
| PA27 | Actual core pointer/storage validity and layout consumers | Reconcile existing raw/reference/union/byte-storage outcomes and live unsupported backing families. Keep runtime unsafe permission distinct from CTFE validity. |
| PA28 | Source-used memory/numeric/atomic intrinsics and metadata operations | Match active source declarations to implemented operations and backend evidence; retain other required intrinsic/atomic families in the release inventory. |
| PA29 | Encoded provided/generic bodies, fresh consumers and downstream republication | Audit generic/lifetime/trait/default/const transport and deterministic public metadata, then generate the complete text/export reference set. The first native Holder call alone does not close this PA. |
| PA30 | Foreign VTable now; genuine allocator/platform ABI and linkage consumers next | Reconcile remaining representations, callable/static/linkage contracts and native interoperability evidence with the handout. |
| PA31/32 | Actual bindings, then unchanged whole core through checking/export/object/consumer | Reconcile active source features and bindings against the complete configured crate. Reducer/header successes remain prerequisites, not whole-core acceptance. |

The integrator owns publication reconciliation; each worker updates the existing
owning handout and declared outcomes as its consumer passes. Reuse independent
evidence whose inputs remain identical. Full cumulative release qualification
starts once semantic and reference debts are closed, not at every helper commit.

## Prioritized issues and assignment ownership

"Owner PA" identifies the compiler/course responsibility. "Test PA" is the
earliest assignment owning **all** features in the complete reproduction. A
source-library reproduction using `no_core`, lang items or compiler-private
attributes belongs in PA31 even when its underlying algorithm has an earlier
owner. Add an earlier-PA regression too when the failure can be reproduced
without those later features. Do not move fixtures mechanically by subsystem.

## Reproductions, tests and closure criteria

Paths below include unpublished worker fixtures. Their presence in a working
branch is not a passing test or a released assignment contract.

- **LIB-001:** `pa31/tests/spec/declarative-macros/` contains predeclared source
  contracts and independent observations for concise/braced declarations,
  forward/imported aliases, repeated introduced items, mixed transparency,
  captured path segments and privacy. Maintainer suites `declarative_macros`,
  `hygiene`, `textual_scopes` and the PA7/PA8 suites retain legacy behavior.
  Close only after the real positive/negative source corpus and native answers
  pass, including exact-context import wakeups, then rerun original core.
- **LIB-002:** extend that PA31 fixture cluster with predeclared captured/literal
  field names, repeated opaque containers, method/associated-name collisions
  and private-member opponents. These new cases are **required, not yet
  qualified**. Reuse PA12 aggregate and PA19 method controls. Close only when
  actual member selection and privacy consume the shared typed context rule;
  a context-aware storage table alone is insufficient.
- **LIB-003:** retain
  `pa31/tests/spec/trait-default-effects/projections/provider-moved.rs` and
  `provider-partial-container.rs`, their public-context counterparts and
  independent contracts. Add actual body-alias/foreign-environment tests,
  independent `'a`/`'b`, complete trait/family arguments, nested binders,
  unresolved inference and failed-budget controls. The private
  `body::defining_cleanups` and trait-selection tests must pass alongside the
  retained native contexts. Do not fix ambiguity by merging arbitrary matches.
- **LIB-004:** keep the unchanged
  `pa31/tests/spec/const-eval-select-ctfe/reference-anonymous-selector.rs` and
  its independent Rust acceptance. The bounded `read_frontier` invariant now
  observes a canonical `u8` read and false Copy decision despite the actual impl.
  The retained 54-event trace resolves all ten required breakpoints: both exact
  queries use the same environment, no reuse cache, and an actual positive header;
  no validation completion occurs before the error. Pending header completion is
  already finished, so waiting on that dependency alone cannot fix the bug. Add
  validated-implementation dependencies and cold/warm/declaration-order controls. Close
  only with correct dependency resumption and a successful complete program;
  returning Deferred forever is not a fix.
- **LIB-005:** preserve `reference-anonymous-selector.rs` and its exact native
  `7 1` result. The complete owner/cached-result/foreign-Code controls now pass
  combined functional qualification. General generic/imported anonymous callers
  and wider reference/conditional callback profiles remain required.
- **LIB-006:** use the actual
  `pa31/tests/spec/source-header-transport/semantic-provider-qualified.rs`
  collector and private `qualified_source_rows`, `transport_domains` and
  `regional_forest` controls. Add same-arity wrong signatures, changed reference

  width/depth/shared-DAG work and storage bounds.
- **LIB-007:** extend PA29's multi-crate contracts and the PA31 source-header
  transport family. Require complete provider → artifact → fresh consumer
  checking → native execution, input reordering, duplicate/missing dependencies,
  tampered owners/roles, const permissions, lifetime/loan/cleanup proof and
  deterministic text views. Support indexes or serialized bytes alone do not
  close this issue. Exported macro provenance is a separately required subtask.
- **LIB-008–012:** each actual build attempt supplies a versioned source/config/
  dependency lock, exact diagnostic, opened-source inventory and artifact/native
  result. Add a reduced owning-PA fixture for every newly reached failure while
  retaining the unchanged whole-crate acceptance test. An earlier parser error
  must not make a later semantic negative appear to pass.
- **LIB-013:** `pa31/tests/spec/source-panic-macros/contract.json` (original source

Every completed fix includes its owning handout update, independently declared
positive/negative outcomes, relevant maintainer tests, and manifest-discovered
text-oracle tests when that assignment's contract is published. Generate and
review oracle text; never hand-edit it or accept an empty/planned suite as a pass.
Text fixtures belong in the earliest PA owning all features, and private test
roots must remain registered in the existing maintainer suite groups.

## Required composite conditional const cleanup

PA25 `const-callables` and PA31 `source-runtime` retain the actual conditional
composite definition prerequisite separately from the CI no-drop repair. The
ordinary source `pa31/tests/spec/source-const-cleanup/impro3.rs` has real
`T: [const] Destruct` and `U: [const] Destruct` premises and moves both actual
`Pair<T,U>` fields into its tuple result. Source SHA-256
`515f0c13f2333d3cb041e83ebe4e2fd36e8252122ecb3a7bd1df9485938dabeb`
is independently accepted by pinned Rust, as are both actual client sources in
`impfr3.json`. Candidate `repair-public4` stops before export at file0 byte2155:
`active cleanup requires const Destruct in its actual defining environment`.
This is a required positive, not an import result or a parity negative. The
[repair evidence](parallel/source-const-repair.md) binds the original prediction,
setup corrections, reference receipts and candidate diagnostics. Explicit
unconditional const premises in `impro4.rs` are a separate control; they do not
replace the conditional requirement. Close it through genuine defining
conditional/field eligibility and normal source/region/loan proof, without
relaxing generic conformance or the interpreter cleanup fence. Selected enum
leaves, actual CTFE cleanup and provider-policy transport remain separate work.

## Work and qualification rules

Keep the two existing GPT-6.1-Sol/high workers. A worker can carry multiple
independent issues, but keeps coherent commits and an explicit handoff for each.
Agree shared APIs before concurrent edits; use separate worktrees. Current
primary lanes and next executable exits are in the current execution queue above.
These are independent blockers; give integration/qualification priority and
do not add workers or worktrees.

Source work proceeds concurrently. Keep at most two heavy jobs globally, one
per tree, two Cargo/test threads,16-GiB absolute output caps and the150-GiB free
reserve. The controller's historical three-slot capacity does not authorize a
third job. Preserve warm caches and current pins; scope cleanup to the owning
tree. Keep measurements exclusive.
Use focused batched checks during a fix and combined focused checks at integration.
Long release qualification runs in scheduled/manual CI and at actual assignment
release, not after each integration. Reuse unchanged qualification and independent
observations with exact source/tool bindings; retain failures and clean outputs.

Update this tracker at each handoff/integration and each original crate attempt.
Report three separate outcomes: **issue fixed**, **crate boundary advanced**,
and **assignment released**. Closing an issue requires its complete reproduction
and guards to pass. Advancing a crate requires a fresh unchanged-crate result.
Releasing a PA requires its entire cumulative contract; no partial activation.

The const/visibility integration passes ten exact frontend/CLI controls in170.23 seconds and six private controls in210.82 seconds, including all nine failures from full CI40424397404. The visibility repair shares normalized type/schema visitation across public interfaces, checks exposed public nominal policies and leaves unrelated hidden types untouched. Its corrected single-charge work is404/976/3816 at8/32/128; ordinary packages without a library inventory do no interface work. Full CI is rerun after publication; no original target artifact or additional PA is complete.
